# Window functions (window.c). The first blocks are hand-written cases for
# the paths that meet select.c (views, subqueries, joins, compounds, DML,
# ALTER); the rest are queries taken from ref/sqlite/test/window*.test
# (window3.test sampled). Multi-row VALUES statements are left out: their
# bytecode differs for an unrelated reason (the VALUES co-routine).
# window functions: hand-written cases for the select.c integration
CREATE TABLE t1(a INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, b INT, c TEXT, d REAL);
CREATE INDEX t1b ON t1(b);
CREATE INDEX t1cb ON t1(c, b);
CREATE TABLE t2(x INT, y TEXT COLLATE NOCASE, z);
CREATE TABLE t3(k INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, v);
WITH RECURSIVE n(i) AS (SELECT 1 UNION ALL SELECT i+1 FROM n WHERE i<60)
INSERT INTO t1 SELECT i, i%7, 'c'||(i%5), CASE WHEN i%4 THEN i*1.25 END FROM n;
WITH RECURSIVE n(i) AS (SELECT 1 UNION ALL SELECT i+1 FROM n WHERE i<40)
INSERT INTO t2 SELECT i%9, CASE i%3 WHEN 0 THEN 'Abc' WHEN 1 THEN 'abc' ELSE 'xyz' END, CASE WHEN i%6 THEN i END FROM n;
INSERT INTO t3 VALUES(1,'one'),(2,'two'),(3,NULL),(4,'four'),(5,'five');
CREATE VIEW v1 AS SELECT a, b, sum(a) OVER (PARTITION BY b ORDER BY a) AS s FROM t1;
CREATE VIEW v2 AS SELECT b, count(*) AS n, rank() OVER (ORDER BY count(*) DESC) AS r FROM t1 GROUP BY b;
==
SELECT a, b, row_number() OVER (ORDER BY b, a) FROM t1 ORDER BY b, a
SELECT a, b, row_number() OVER (ORDER BY b, a) FROM t1 ORDER BY a
SELECT a, b, rank() OVER (PARTITION BY c ORDER BY b) FROM t1 ORDER BY c, b
SELECT b, sum(a) OVER (PARTITION BY b) FROM t1 WHERE a < 20
SELECT DISTINCT b, count(*) OVER (PARTITION BY b) FROM t1
SELECT a, sum(a) OVER (ORDER BY a) FROM t1 ORDER BY a DESC LIMIT 5
SELECT a, sum(a) OVER (ORDER BY a) FROM t1 LIMIT 5 OFFSET 10
SELECT a, sum(a) OVER (ORDER BY a) AS s FROM t1 ORDER BY s DESC LIMIT 3
SELECT * FROM v1 WHERE b = 3
SELECT * FROM v1 WHERE s > 100 ORDER BY a
SELECT * FROM v2
SELECT * FROM v2 WHERE r < 3
SELECT b, s FROM (SELECT b, sum(a) OVER (PARTITION BY b) AS s FROM t1) WHERE b > 4
SELECT x, y FROM (SELECT b AS x, row_number() OVER (ORDER BY a) AS y FROM t1) WHERE y BETWEEN 3 AND 6
SELECT t3.v, w.r FROM t3 JOIN (SELECT a, rank() OVER (ORDER BY d) AS r FROM t1) AS w ON w.a = t3.k
SELECT k, (SELECT sum(a) OVER () FROM t1 WHERE b = k LIMIT 1) FROM t3
SELECT k, (SELECT max(r) FROM (SELECT row_number() OVER (ORDER BY a) AS r FROM t1 WHERE b = t3.k)) FROM t3
SELECT k FROM t3 WHERE k IN (SELECT row_number() OVER (ORDER BY a) FROM t1 WHERE b = 2)
SELECT k FROM t3 WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM (SELECT lag(a) OVER (ORDER BY a) AS l FROM t1) WHERE l = k)
WITH w AS (SELECT a, b, ntile(4) OVER (ORDER BY a) AS q FROM t1) SELECT q, count(*), sum(b) FROM w GROUP BY q
WITH w AS MATERIALIZED (SELECT a, dense_rank() OVER (ORDER BY b) AS dr FROM t1) SELECT * FROM w WHERE dr = 2
SELECT b, count(*), sum(count(*)) OVER (ORDER BY b) FROM t1 GROUP BY b
SELECT b, sum(a), rank() OVER (ORDER BY sum(a) DESC) FROM t1 GROUP BY b HAVING count(*) > 8
SELECT c, max(b), first_value(c) OVER (ORDER BY max(b), c) FROM t1 GROUP BY c
SELECT count(*) OVER () FROM t1 GROUP BY b
SELECT sum(a) OVER (), count(*) FROM t1
SELECT a, b, x FROM t1 JOIN t2 ON b = x WHERE a < 6 ORDER BY a, x
SELECT a, x, row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY a ORDER BY x, z) FROM t1 JOIN t2 ON b = x WHERE a < 6
SELECT a, x, sum(z) OVER (PARTITION BY a ORDER BY z ROWS 1 PRECEDING) FROM t1 LEFT JOIN t2 ON b = x AND z > 30 WHERE a < 8
SELECT a, row_number() OVER (ORDER BY a) FROM t1 WHERE a < 4 UNION ALL SELECT k, rank() OVER (ORDER BY v) FROM t3
SELECT a, row_number() OVER (ORDER BY a) FROM t1 WHERE a < 4 UNION SELECT k, rank() OVER (ORDER BY v) FROM t3 ORDER BY 1, 2
SELECT b FROM t1 WHERE a < 10 INTERSECT SELECT row_number() OVER () FROM t3
SELECT y, z, rank() OVER (ORDER BY y) FROM t2 ORDER BY y, z
SELECT y, z, rank() OVER (ORDER BY y COLLATE BINARY) FROM t2 ORDER BY y COLLATE BINARY, z
SELECT y, count(*) OVER (PARTITION BY y) FROM t2 ORDER BY 1, 2
SELECT y, group_concat(z) OVER (PARTITION BY y ORDER BY z RANGE BETWEEN 5 PRECEDING AND 5 FOLLOWING) FROM t2
SELECT y, group_concat(z) OVER (PARTITION BY y ORDER BY z DESC RANGE BETWEEN 5 PRECEDING AND 5 FOLLOWING) FROM t2
SELECT y, group_concat(z) OVER (ORDER BY z NULLS LAST RANGE BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 3 FOLLOWING) FROM t2
SELECT y, group_concat(z) OVER (ORDER BY z DESC NULLS FIRST RANGE BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) FROM t2
SELECT y, group_concat(z) OVER (ORDER BY z NULLS LAST RANGE BETWEEN 2 FOLLOWING AND 6 FOLLOWING) FROM t2
SELECT y, group_concat(z) OVER (ORDER BY z DESC NULLS FIRST RANGE BETWEEN 6 PRECEDING AND 2 PRECEDING) FROM t2
SELECT y, count(*) OVER (ORDER BY y RANGE BETWEEN 1 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) FROM t2
SELECT d, sum(d) OVER (ORDER BY d RANGE BETWEEN 2.5 PRECEDING AND 1.5 FOLLOWING) FROM t1 WHERE a < 20
SELECT d, sum(d) OVER (ORDER BY d RANGE BETWEEN 2.5 PRECEDING AND 1.5 PRECEDING) FROM t1 WHERE a < 20
SELECT d, max(d) OVER (ORDER BY a ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING), min(d) OVER (ORDER BY a ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) FROM t1 WHERE a < 25
SELECT c, max(c) OVER (ORDER BY a ROWS 3 PRECEDING), min(c COLLATE NOCASE) OVER (ORDER BY a ROWS BETWEEN 1 FOLLOWING AND 3 FOLLOWING) FROM t1 WHERE a < 15
SELECT y, max(y) OVER (ORDER BY x ROWS BETWEEN 1 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING), min(y) OVER (ORDER BY x, z GROUPS BETWEEN 1 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) FROM t2

SELECT a, b, nth_value(a, 2) OVER (PARTITION BY c ORDER BY b ROWS BETWEEN 1 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING EXCLUDE CURRENT ROW) FROM t1 WHERE a < 30
SELECT a, b, first_value(a) OVER (PARTITION BY c ORDER BY b GROUPS CURRENT ROW EXCLUDE GROUP), last_value(a) OVER (PARTITION BY c ORDER BY b GROUPS CURRENT ROW EXCLUDE GROUP) FROM t1 WHERE a < 30
SELECT a, nth_value(a, b+1) OVER (ORDER BY a) FROM t1 WHERE a < 15

SELECT a, lead(a, b) OVER (ORDER BY a), lag(c, 3, 'none') OVER (PARTITION BY b ORDER BY a), lead(d, 1, a) OVER (ORDER BY a DESC) FROM t1 WHERE a < 20

SELECT a, row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY b), count(*) OVER (PARTITION BY b, c) FROM t1 WHERE a < 25 ORDER BY a

SELECT a, sum(a) OVER w1, avg(a) OVER w2, total(a) OVER w1 FROM t1 WHERE a < 25 WINDOW w1 AS (ORDER BY a ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 3 FOLLOWING), w2 AS (w1)
SELECT a, sum(a) OVER (w1 ROWS 1 PRECEDING), count(*) OVER (w1 RANGE BETWEEN 1 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) FROM t1 WHERE a < 25 WINDOW w1 AS (PARTITION BY b ORDER BY a)
SELECT a, sum(a) OVER (w2 ORDER BY a) FROM t1 WHERE a < 25 WINDOW w1 AS (PARTITION BY c), w2 AS (w1)
SELECT a, count(*) FILTER (WHERE b > 3) OVER (ORDER BY a), sum(a) FILTER (WHERE c = 'c1') OVER (PARTITION BY b ORDER BY a ROWS 2 PRECEDING) FROM t1 WHERE a < 25
SELECT a, max(a) FILTER (WHERE b % 2) OVER (ORDER BY a ROWS BETWEEN 1 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) FROM t1 WHERE a < 25
SELECT a, group_concat(c, '-') FILTER (WHERE a % 3 = 0) OVER (ORDER BY a GROUPS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING EXCLUDE CURRENT ROW) FROM t1 WHERE a < 25
SELECT a, sum(a) OVER (ORDER BY a ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 PRECEDING), sum(a) OVER (ORDER BY a ROWS BETWEEN 1 FOLLOWING AND 3 FOLLOWING) FROM t1 WHERE a < 15

# ALTER TABLE with window functions in views and triggers
CREATE TABLE t1(a INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, b INT, c TEXT);
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(1,1,'x'),(2,1,'y'),(3,2,'z');
CREATE VIEW v1 AS SELECT a, sum(b) OVER (PARTITION BY c ORDER BY a ROWS 1 PRECEDING) AS s, row_number() OVER w FROM t1 WINDOW w AS (ORDER BY b, c);
CREATE TABLE log(x);
CREATE TRIGGER tr AFTER INSERT ON t1 BEGIN INSERT INTO log SELECT max(b) FILTER (WHERE c > 'a') OVER (ORDER BY a) FROM t1; END;
==
ALTER TABLE t1 RENAME COLUMN b TO bb
ALTER TABLE t1 RENAME COLUMN c TO cc
ALTER TABLE t1 RENAME TO t2
ALTER TABLE t1 RENAME COLUMN a TO aa
SELECT * FROM v1
----
# window1.test
CREATE TABLE t1(a, b, c, d);
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(1, 2, 3, 4);
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(5, 6, 7, 8);
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(9, 10, 11, 12);
==
SELECT sum(b) OVER () FROM t1
SELECT a, sum(b) OVER () FROM t1
SELECT a, 4 + sum(b) OVER () FROM t1
SELECT a + 4 + sum(b) OVER () FROM t1
SELECT a, sum(b) OVER (PARTITION BY c) FROM t1
----
# window1.test
CREATE TABLE t1(a, b, c, d);
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(1, 2, 3, 4);
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(5, 6, 7, 8);
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(9, 10, 11, 12);
CREATE TABLE t2(a, b, c);
INSERT INTO t2 VALUES(0, 0, 0);
INSERT INTO t2 VALUES(1, 1, 1);
INSERT INTO t2 VALUES(2, 0, 2);
INSERT INTO t2 VALUES(3, 1, 0);
INSERT INTO t2 VALUES(4, 0, 1);
INSERT INTO t2 VALUES(5, 1, 2);
INSERT INTO t2 VALUES(6, 0, 0);
==
SELECT a, sum(a) OVER (PARTITION BY b) FROM t2
SELECT a, sum(a) OVER (PARTITION BY b) FROM t2 ORDER BY a
SELECT a, sum(a) OVER () FROM t2 ORDER BY a
SELECT a, sum(a) OVER (ORDER BY a) FROM t2
SELECT a, sum(a) OVER (PARTITION BY b ORDER BY a) FROM t2 ORDER BY a
SELECT a, sum(a) OVER (PARTITION BY c ORDER BY a) FROM t2 ORDER BY a
SELECT a, sum(a) OVER (PARTITION BY b ORDER BY a DESC) FROM t2 ORDER BY a
SELECT a, sum(a) OVER (PARTITION BY b ORDER BY a DESC), sum(a) OVER (PARTITION BY c ORDER BY a) FROM t2 ORDER BY a
SELECT a, sum(a) OVER (ORDER BY a), avg(a) OVER (ORDER BY a) FROM t2 ORDER BY a
SELECT a, count() OVER (ORDER BY a DESC), string_agg(a, '.') OVER (ORDER BY a DESC) FROM t2 ORDER BY a DESC
SELECT a, count(*) OVER (ORDER BY a DESC), group_concat(a, '.') OVER (ORDER BY a DESC) FROM t2 ORDER BY a DESC
SELECT ntile(0) OVER (ORDER BY a) FROM t2
SELECT ntile(-1) OVER (ORDER BY a) FROM t2
SELECT ntile('zbc') OVER (ORDER BY a) FROM t2

INSERT INTO t2 VALUES(4, 0, 1);
INSERT INTO t2 VALUES(5, 1, 2);
INSERT INTO t2 VALUES(6, 0, 0);
CREATE TABLE t4(a, b);

SELECT ntile(1) OVER (ORDER BY a) FROM t4

# window1.test
CREATE TABLE t1(x);
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(7), (6), (5), (4), (3), (2), (1);
CREATE TABLE t2(x);
INSERT INTO t2 VALUES('b'), ('a');
==
SELECT x, count(*) OVER (ORDER BY x) FROM t1
SELECT * FROM t2, (SELECT x, count(*) OVER (ORDER BY x) FROM t1) ORDER BY 1, 2
SELECT x, lag(x) FILTER (WHERE (x%2)=0) OVER w FROM t1 WINDOW w AS (ORDER BY x)

# window1.test
CREATE TABLE t1(x, y);
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(1, 2);
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(3, 4);
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(5, 6);
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(7, 8);
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(9, 10);
==
SELECT nth_value(x, 1) FROM t1
SELECT * FROM t1 WHERE nth_value(x, 1) OVER (ORDER BY y)
SELECT count(*) FROM t1 GROUP BY y HAVING nth_value(x, 1) OVER (ORDER BY y)
SELECT count(*) FROM t1 GROUP BY nth_value(x, 1) OVER (ORDER BY y)
SELECT count(*) FROM t1 LIMIT nth_value(x, 1) OVER ()
SELECT trim(x) OVER (ORDER BY y) FROM t1
SELECT max(x) OVER abc FROM t1 WINDOW def AS (ORDER BY y)
SELECT row_number(x) OVER () FROM t1
SELECT lead(y) OVER win, lead(y, 2) OVER win, lead(y, 3, 'default') OVER win FROM t1 WINDOW win AS (ORDER BY x)
SELECT row_number() OVER (ORDER BY x) FROM t1
SELECT row_number() OVER win, lead(x) OVER win FROM t1 WINDOW win AS (ORDER BY x ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW)

# window1.test

CREATE VIEW v2 AS SELECT sum(b) OVER win, min(b) OVER win, max(b) OVER win FROM t3 WINDOW win AS (ORDER BY c);

INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(2, 'even', 'two', 2);

==
SELECT string_agg(c, '.') OVER (PARTITION BY b ORDER BY c) FROM t1
SELECT group_concat(c, '.') OVER (win1 ORDER BY c) FROM t1 WINDOW win1 AS (PARTITION BY b)
SELECT string_agg(c, '.') OVER win2 FROM t1 WINDOW win1 AS (PARTITION BY b), win2 AS (win1 ORDER BY c)
SELECT group_concat(c, '.') OVER (win2) FROM t1 WINDOW win1 AS (PARTITION BY b), win2 AS (win1 ORDER BY c)
SELECT string_agg(c, '.') OVER win5 FROM t1 WINDOW win1 AS (PARTITION BY b), win2 AS (win1), win3 AS (win2), win4 AS (win3), win5 AS (win4 ORDER BY c)

SELECT max(b) OVER ( ORDER BY a ) FROM t2
SELECT row_number() OVER ( ORDER BY a RANGE BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW ) FROM t2
SELECT dense_rank() OVER ( ORDER BY b RANGE BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW ) FROM t2
SELECT dense_rank() OVER ( PARTITION BY b%10 ORDER BY b RANGE BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW ) FROM t2
SELECT rank() OVER ( PARTITION BY b%10 ORDER BY a RANGE BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW ) FROM t2
SELECT rank() OVER ( ORDER BY b%10 RANGE BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW ) FROM t2
SELECT last_value(a+b) OVER ( ORDER BY a RANGE BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW ) FROM t2

SELECT last_value(a+b) OVER (PARTITION BY b%2,a ORDER BY b%10 RANGE BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) FROM t2
SELECT nth_value(b,b+1) OVER (PARTITION BY b%10 ORDER BY a RANGE BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) FROM t2
SELECT nth_value(b,b+1) OVER ( ORDER BY b,a RANGE BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW ) FROM t2
SELECT nth_value(b,b+1) OVER (PARTITION BY b%2,a ORDER BY b%10 RANGE BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) FROM t2
SELECT first_value(b) OVER (ORDER BY a RANGE BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) FROM t2
SELECT first_value(b) OVER (PARTITION BY b%10 ORDER BY a RANGE BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) FROM t2

SELECT group_concat(CAST(b AS TEXT), '.') OVER (win1 RANGE BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) FROM t2 WINDOW win1 AS (PARTITION BY b%2,a ORDER BY b%10) ORDER BY 1

SELECT lead(b,b) OVER ( ORDER BY b,a ROWS BETWEEN 4 PRECEDING AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING EXCLUDE GROUP ) FROM t2
SELECT lag(b,b) OVER (ORDER BY a ROWS BETWEEN 4 PRECEDING AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING EXCLUDE GROUP) FROM t2
SELECT lag(b,b) OVER ( ORDER BY b,a ROWS BETWEEN 4 PRECEDING AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING EXCLUDE GROUP ) FROM t2
SELECT group_concat(CAST(b AS TEXT), '.') OVER (win1 ORDER BY b%10 ROWS BETWEEN 4 PRECEDING AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING EXCLUDE GROUP) FROM t2 WINDOW win1 AS (PARTITION BY b%2,a) ORDER BY 1
SELECT count(*) OVER win, group_concat(CAST(b AS TEXT), '.') FILTER (WHERE a%2=0) OVER win FROM t2 WINDOW win AS (PARTITION BY (a%10) ORDER BY a ROWS BETWEEN 4 PRECEDING AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING EXCLUDE GROUP)

INSERT INTO t5 VALUES(5, 'A', 'five', 1);

INSERT INTO ttt VALUES(2, 2, 2);

INSERT INTO ttt VALUES(8, 2, 4);
INSERT INTO ttt VALUES(9, 3, 5);

INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(3, 4);
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(5, 6);
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(7, 8);
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(9, 10);
==
SELECT lead(y) OVER win FROM t1 WINDOW win AS (ORDER BY x)
SELECT lead(y, 2) OVER win FROM t1 WINDOW win AS (ORDER BY x)
SELECT lead(y, 3, -1) OVER win FROM t1 WINDOW win AS (ORDER BY x)
SELECT lead(y) OVER win, lead(y) OVER win FROM t1 WINDOW win AS (ORDER BY x)
SELECT lead(y) OVER win, lead(y, 2) OVER win, lead(y, 3, -1) OVER win FROM t1 WINDOW win AS (ORDER BY x)

SELECT row_number() OVER win FROM t3 WINDOW win AS ( ORDER BY c, b, a ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING EXCLUDE NO OTHERS )

SELECT min(c) OVER win, max(c) OVER win, sum(c) OVER win FROM t3 WINDOW win AS ( ORDER BY c, b, a ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW EXCLUDE NO OTHERS ) ORDER BY a, b, c

SELECT nth_value(c, 14) OVER win FROM t3 WINDOW win AS ( ORDER BY c, b, a ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING EXCLUDE GROUP )

SELECT nth_value(c, 14) OVER win FROM t3 WINDOW win AS ( ORDER BY c, b, a ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING EXCLUDE TIES )
SELECT min(c) OVER win, max(c) OVER win, sum(c) OVER win FROM t3 WINDOW win AS ( ORDER BY c, b, a ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW EXCLUDE TIES ) ORDER BY a, b, c

# window8.test

CREATE VIEW v3 AS SELECT b, d, max(d) OVER (PARTITION BY b), row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY b) FROM t1;
